1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The space variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 853 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 339 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 853 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the space structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 339. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
